class="sc-99238b4b-0 cdIwwD"><span id="main-content" tabindex="-1"></span></span><div class="topicMain__container"><div dir="ltr" class="sc-690ca30c-0 cmrsaE"><div class="pageTitle__container"><div class="pageTitle__content"><div class="pageTitle__title"><h1>Standard Specifications for Highway Construction</h1></div></div></div></div><span class="last_Updated_Text"><img src="/icons/last-updated-star.svg" alt=""/> <!-- -->Last updated on April 1, 2026</span><div class="topicContent__main"><div id="introduction" class="styled-h2"><p>Standard specifications for highway construction serves as a standard reference in construction contracts.</p></div><div id="body" class="styled-h2"><p>The specifications outline minimum requirements for highway construction in British Columbia. Where modifications are necessary to suit local conditions they will be specified in special provisions for each contract.</p>

<h2 class="bc_h2"><a id="standard" name="standard"></a>2025 Standard Specifications</h2>

<p class="bc_paragraph">The 2025&nbsp;Standard Specifications for Highway Construction is completed and is adopted February&nbsp;1, 2025.</p>

<ul>
	<li><a href="https://www2.gov.bc.ca/assets/gov/driving-and-transportation/transportation-infrastructure/engineering-standards-and-guidelines/highway-specifications/volume_1_ss2025.pdf" target="_blank">2025 Standard Specifications for Highway Construction Volume 1 (PDF, 32MB)</a>&nbsp;<br />
	February&nbsp;1, 2025</li>
	<li><a href="https://www2.gov.bc.ca/assets/gov/driving-and-transportation/transportation-infrastructure/engineering-standards-and-guidelines/highway-specifications/volume_2_ss2025.pdf" target="_blank">2025&nbsp;Standard Specifications for Highway Construction Volume 2 (PDF, 67MB)</a><br />
	February&nbsp;1, 2025</li>
</ul>

<ul>
</ul>

<h3 class="bc_h3"><a id="Amendments" name="Amendments"></a>Amendments</h3>

<p>Amendments the&nbsp;Standard Specifications are detailed below.</p>

<ul>
	<li><a href="https://www2.gov.bc.ca/assets/gov/driving-and-transportation/transportation-infrastructure/engineering-standards-and-guidelines/highway-specifications/2025-amendments/2025-special-provisions-appendix-amendments.pdf" target="_self">Special Provisions Appendix for Amendments to the 2025 Standard Specifications for Highway Construction&nbsp;(PDF)</a><br />
	<strong>Adopted February 1, 2025</strong><br />
	<strong>Updated April 1, 2026</strong></li>
</ul>
